The full suite exposed one real policy issue: tests and older worlds without `RunState` were being treated as normal-mode resurrection. I’m changing missing run state to preserve legacy permadeath; new games explicitly create `RunState`, so normal mode still resurrects.

    24	  const mode = String(value || fallback).toLowerCase();
    25	  return DEATH_MODE_SET.has(mode) ? mode : fallback;
    26	}
    27	
    28	function findRunStateEntity(world) {
    29	  for (const [id] of world.query(RunState)) return id;
    30	  return 0;
    31	}
    32	
    33	export function ensureRunState(world, opts = {}) {
    34	  let id = findRunStateEntity(world);
    35	  const difficulty = normalizeDifficulty(opts.difficulty);
    36	  const defaultMode = difficulty === "hard" ? DEATH_MODES.permadeath : DEFAULT_NORMAL_DEATH_MODE;
    37	  const deathMode = normalizeDeathMode(opts.deathMode, defaultMode);
    38	  const patch = {
    39	    difficulty,
    40	    deathMode,
    41	    resurrectionCount: Math.max(0, Number(opts.resurrectionCount || 0) | 0),
    42	  };
    43	
    44	  if (id > 0) {
    45	    const current = world.get(id, RunState) || {};
    46	    world.set(id, RunState, {
    47	      ...current,
    48	      difficulty,
    49	      deathMode,
    50	      resurrectionCount: Math.max(0, Number(current.resurrectionCount || 0) | 0),
    51	    });
    52	    return id;
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  id = world.create();
    56	  world.add(id, RunState, patch);
    57	  return id;
    58	}
    59	
    60	export function getRunState(world) {
    61	  const id = findRunStateEntity(world);
    62	  if (id > 0) return { id, state: world.get(id, RunState) };
    63	  return { id: ensureRunState(world), state: world.get(findRunStateEntity(world), RunState) };
    64	}
    65	
    66	export function setDeathMode(world, mode, opts = {}) {
    67	  const normalized = normalizeDeathMode(mode);
    68	  const { id, state } = getRunState(world);
    69	  world.set(id, RunState, {
    70	    ...state,
    71	    deathMode: normalized,
    72	    difficulty: normalizeDifficulty(opts.difficulty || state?.difficulty),
    73	  });
    74	}
    75	
    76	function equippedItemIds(world, actorId) {
    77	  const eq = world.get(actorId, Equipment);
    78	  const out = new Set();
    79	  if (!eq) return out;
    80	  for (const slot of GEAR_SLOTS) {
    81	    const itemId = Number(eq[slot] || 0) | 0;
    82	    if (itemId > 0) out.add(itemId);
    83	  }
    84	  return out;
    85	}
    86	
    87	function clearEquippedSlot(world, actorId, itemId) {
    88	  const eq = world.get(actorId, Equipment);
    89	  if (!eq) return;
    90	  let changed = false;
    91	  const next = { ...eq };
    92	  for (const slot of GEAR_SLOTS) {
    93	    if ((Number(next[slot] || 0) | 0) === itemId) {
    94	      next[slot] = null;
    95	      changed = true;
    96	    }
    97	  }
    98	  if (changed) world.set(actorId, Equipment, next);
    99	}
   100	
   101	function dropItemAt(world, actorId, itemId, x, y, source) {
   102	  if (!(itemId > 0) || !world.isAlive(itemId) || !world.has(itemId, ItemInfo)) return 0;
   103	  removeFromInventory(world, actorId, itemId);
   104	  clearEquippedSlot(world, actorId, itemId);
   105	  const placed = placeOnGround(world, itemId, x, y, { mergeCompatibleAmmo: true });
   106	  const droppedId = placed.itemId || itemId;
   107	  world.emit("item:dropped", {
   108	    actor: actorId,
   109	    itemId: droppedId,
   110	    count: world.get(droppedId, ItemInfo)?.count || 1,
   111	    at: { x, y },
   112	    source,
   113	    origin: { x, y },
   114	  });
   115	  return droppedId;
   116	}
   117	
   118	function chooseKeptEquippedItem(world, actorId, equipped) {
   119	  const eq = world.get(actorId, Equipment);
   120	  if (!eq) return 0;
   121	  for (const slot of ["weapon", "ranged", "armor", "offhand"]) {
   122	    const itemId = Number(eq[slot] || 0) | 0;
   123	    if (equipped.has(itemId)) return itemId;
   124	  }
   125	  for (const itemId of equipped) return itemId;
   126	  return 0;
   127	}
   128	
   129	function dropDeathModeItems(world, actorId, mode, x, y) {
   130	  if (mode === DEATH_MODES.mercy) return [];
   131	
   132	  const dropped = [];
   133	  const equipped = equippedItemIds(world, actorId);
   134	  const keepEquipped = mode === DEATH_MODES.dropBackpack;
   135	  const keptItem = mode === DEATH_MODES.dropAllButOne
   136	    ? chooseKeptEquippedItem(world, actorId, equipped)
   137	    : 0;
   138	
   139	  for (const itemId of inventoryItems(world, actorId)) {
   140	    if (keepEquipped && equipped.has(itemId)) continue;
   141	    if (mode === DEATH_MODES.dropAllButOne && equipped.has(itemId)) continue;
   142	    if (itemId === keptItem) continue;
   143	    const droppedId = dropItemAt(world, actorId, itemId, x, y, "resurrection");
   144	    if (droppedId > 0) dropped.push(droppedId);
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  if (mode === DEATH_MODES.dropAllButOne) {
   148	    for (const itemId of equipped) {
   149	      if (itemId === keptItem) continue;
   150	      const droppedId = dropItemAt(world, actorId, itemId, x, y, "resurrection");
   151	      if (droppedId > 0) dropped.push(droppedId);
   152	    }
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  return dropped;
   156	}
   157	
   158	/**
   159	 * Convert a fatal player hit into a pseudo-death if the current run mode allows it.
   160	 *
   161	 * @returns {null|{mode:string,count:number,droppedItemIds:number[],returnTicket:{depth:number,x:number,y:number}}}
   162	 */
   163	export function tryHandlePlayerPseudoDeath(world, actorId, death = {}) {
   164	  const id = Number(actorId) | 0;
   165	  if (!(id > 0) || !world.isAlive(id) || !world.has(id, Player)) return null;
   166	
   167	  const runId = findRunStateEntity(world);
   168	  if (!(runId > 0)) return null;
   169	  const state = world.get(runId, RunState);
   170	  const mode = normalizeDeathMode(state?.deathMode);
   171	  if (mode === DEATH_MODES.permadeath) return null;
   172	
   173	  const pos = world.get(id, Position);
   174	  const vit = world.get(id, Vitality);
   175	  if (!pos || !vit) return null;
   176	
   177	  const returnTicket = {
   178	    depth: 0,
   179	    x: pos.x | 0,
   180	    y: pos.y | 0,
   181	  };
   182	  for (const [, ds] of world.query(DungeonState)) {
   183	    returnTicket.depth = Number(ds?.currentDepth || 0) | 0;
   184	    break;
   185	  }
   186	
   187	  const droppedItemIds = dropDeathModeItems(world, id, mode, returnTicket.x, returnTicket.y);
   188	  const resurrectHp = Math.max(1, Math.floor((Number(vit.maxHp) || 1) * 0.35));
   189	  vit.hp = Math.min(Number(vit.maxHp) || resurrectHp, resurrectHp);
   190	
   191	  const count = Math.max(0, Number(state?.resurrectionCount || 0) | 0) + 1;
   192	  world.set(runId, RunState, {
   193	    ...state,
   194	    deathMode: mode,
   195	    resurrectionCount: count,
   196	  });
   197	
   198	  const detail = {
   199	    id,
   200	    mode,
   201	    count,
   202	    killer: Number(death.killer || 0) | 0,
   203	    cause: String(death.cause || "unknown"),
   204	    droppedItemIds,
   205	    returnTicket,
   206	    hp: vit.hp | 0,
   207	    maxHp: vit.maxHp | 0,
   208	  };
   209	  world.emit("player:resurrected", detail);
   210	  world.emit("dungeon:teleport-depth", {
   211	    actor: id,
   212	    source: "resurrection",
   213	    targetDepth: 0,
   214	    returnTicket,
   215	  });
   216	
   217	  return detail;
   218	}

> AGENT

Implemented death modes with normal-mode resurrection and hard-mode permadeath.

Core changes:
- Added `RunState` with `deathMode` and `resurrectionCount` in [RunState.js](/home/pjensen/Repos/JSHack/src/rules/components/RunState.js:3).
- Added death mode policy in [deathModes.js](/home/pjensen/Repos/JSHack/src/rules/utils/deathModes.js:33):
  - `permadeath`
  - `mercy`
  - `drop_backpack`
  - `drop_all_but_one`
- Wired fatal player damage through pseudo-death before `died` fires in [dealDamage.js](/home/pjensen/Repos/JSHack/src/rules/utils/dealDamage.js:465).
- New games now set hard to `permadeath` and normal to `drop_backpack` in [main.js](/home/pjensen/Repos/JSHack/src/main.js:870).
- Resurrection emits a town depth teleport with a return ticket, and transition wiring treats `source: "resurrection"` like Homecoming so the return portal is created in town in [transitionWiring.js](/home/pjensen/Repos/JSHack/src/main/wiring/transitionWiring.js:253).

Behavior now:
- Hard mode: real death, save clears, tombstone/death screen still happen.
- Normal mode: fatal hit restores 35% HP, increments `resurrectionCount`, drops backpack on the death tile, keeps equipped gear, returns player to town, and creates a return portal to the death tile.
- Missing `RunState` preserves legacy permadeath, so old isolated tests/worlds do not silently become softcore.

Verification:
- Passed: `deno test --allow-read tests/deathModes.test.mjs tests/deitySystem.test.mjs tests/tombstoneKillerAttribution.test.mjs tests/dealDamage.test.mjs tests/savegameWiring.test.mjs tests/deathShareWiring.test.mjs tests/useItemDataDriven.test.mjs`
- Passed: architecture guard tests and `agent-health`.
- Full suite attempt hit existing Deno `localStorage` database errors in transition tests (`unable to open database file`); rerunning with `--allow-write` did not change that.
